</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v44" data-v="44"> And the next Shabbat &#91;rest&#93; day came almost the whole city together to hear the word of God-The Father.</span>

</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v48" data-v="48"> And when the Goyim &#91;Gentiles&#93; heard this, they were glad, and glorified the word of the Lord - Kurios: and as many as were ordained to eternal life believed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v49" data-v="49"> And the word of the Lord - Kurios was published throughout all the region.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v50" data-v="50"> But the Y’hudim (Judeans) &#91;praised of Yah&#93; stirred up the devout and honourable women, and the chief men of the city, and raised persecution against Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; and Bar-Nabba &#91;son of rest&#93;, and expelled them out of their coasts.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Luke 24:49; John 14:16; John 15:26"> But the multitude of the city was divided: and part held with the Y’hudim (Judeans) &#91;praised of Yah&#93;, and part with the apostles.</span>

<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Gosel 3:11; Acts 11:15–16; Luke 3:16"> And when there was an assault made both of the Goyim &#91;Gentiles&#93;, and also of the Y’hudim (Judeans) &#91;praised of Yah&#93; with their rulers, to use &#91;them&#93; despitefully, and to stone them,</span>

We also are men of like passions with you, and preach unto you that you2f should turn from these vanities unto the living God-The Father, which made heaven, and earth, and the sea, and all things that are in it:</span>

<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Acts 2:22; Gosel 28:15; Acts 21:40"> And there came there &#91;certain&#93; Y’hudim (Judeans) &#91;praised of Yah&#93; from Antioch &#91;speedy as a chariot&#93; and Iconium &#91;little image&#93;, who persuaded the people, and, having stoned Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93;, drew &#91;him&#93; out of the city, supposing he had been dead.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="John 15:27; Luke 10:1–2; Numbers 27:17"> And when they had preached the Gospel (joyful message) to that city, and had taught many, they returned again to Lystra &#91;ransoming&#93;, and &#91;to&#93; Iconium &#91;little image&#93;, and Antioch &#91;speedy as a chariot&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Acts 1:2; Acts 4:33; Acts 1:8–9"> Confirming the souls of the disciples, &#91;and&#93; exhorting them to continue in the faith, and that we must through much tribulation enter into the kingdom of God-The Father.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Acts 15:22; Acts 1:26"> And when they had ordained them elders in every church, and had prayed with fasting, they commended them to the Lord - Kurios, on whom they believed.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="1 Samuel 16:7; Acts 15:8; Hebrews 4:13"> And after they had passed throughout Pisidia &#91;pitchy&#93;, they came to Pamphylia &#91;of every tribe&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="1 Corinthians 9:2; John 6:70–71; John 17:12"> And when they had preached the word in Perga &#91;earthy&#93;, they went down into Attalia:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Leviticus 16:8; Joshua 18:10; 1 Samuel 14:41–42"> And there sailed to Antioch &#91;speedy as a chariot&#93;, from what place they had been recommended to the grace of God-The Father for the work which they fulfilled.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> And when they were come, and had gathered the church together, they rehearsed all that God-The Father had done with them, and how he had opened the door of faith unto the Goyim &#91;Gentiles&#93;.</span>
</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Luke 1:3; Luke 24:19; Gosel 4:23–24"> And certain men which came down from Y’hudah &#91;praised&#93; taught the brethren, &#91;and said&#93;, Except you2f be circumcised after the manner of Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, you2f cannot be saved.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Acts 1:9; John 20:21; Isaiah 59:20–21"> When therefore Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; and Bar-Nabba &#91;son of rest&#93; had no small dissension and disputation with them, they determined that Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; and Bar-Nabba &#91;son of rest&#93;, and certain other of them, should go up to Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; unto the apostles and elders about this question.</span>

<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="1 Corinthians 15:5–7; Acts 13:31; John 20:26"> And being brought on their way by the church, they passed through Phoenicia &#91;land of palm trees&#93; and Shomron &#91;watch mountain&#93;, declaring the conversion of the Goyim &#91;Gentiles&#93;: and they caused great joy unto all the brethren.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Luke 24:49; John 14:16; John 15:26"> And when they were come to Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, they were received of the church, and &#91;of&#93; the apostles and elders, and they declared all things that God-The Father had done with them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Gosel 3:11; Acts 11:15–16; Luke 3:16"> But there rose up certain of the sect of the P’rushim (Pharisees) &#91;separatists&#93; which believed, saying, That it was needful to circumcise them, and to command &#91;them&#93; to keep the law of Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Luke 19:11; Luke 17:20; Daniel 7:27"> And the apostles and elders came together for to consider of this matter.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Gosel 24:36; 1 Thessalonians 5:1–2; Mark 13:32"> And when there had been much disputing, Kefa &#91;pebble&#93; rose up, and said unto them, Men &#91;and&#93; brethren, you2f know how that a good while ago God-The Father made choice among us, that the Goyim &#91;Gentiles&#93; by my mouth should hear the word of the Gospel (joyful message), and believe.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Gosel 28:19; Mark 16:15; Romans 15:19"> And God-The Father, which knows the hearts, bore them witness, giving them the Holy Spirit &#91;Ruach HaKodesh&#93;, even as &#91;he did&#93; unto us;</span>

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Luke 24:4; John 20:12; Revelation 7:14"> Now therefore why tempt you2f God-The Father, to put a yoke upon the neck of the disciples, which neither our fathers nor we were able to bear?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Revelation 1:7; 1 Thessalonians 4:16; Mark 13:26"> But we believe that through the grace of the Lord - Kurios &#91;he to whom a person belongs&#93; Yeshua &#91;God is Salvation&#93; Moshiach &#91;Messiah&#93; we shall be saved, even as they.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Luke 24:52; Gosel 21:1; Luke 24:50"> Then all the multitude kept silence, and gave audience to Bar-Nabba &#91;son of rest&#93; and Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93;, declaring what miracles and wonders God-The Father had wrought among the Goyim &#91;Gentiles&#93; by them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Acts 20:8; Luke 22:12; Mark 14:15"> And after they had held their peace, Ya’akov &#91;heal-grabber&#93; answered, saying, Men &#91;and&#93; brethren, hearken unto me:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Acts 6:4; Ephesians 6:18; Colossians 4:2"> Shim’on &#91;he who hears&#93; has declared how God-The Father at the first did visit the Goyim &#91;Gentiles&#93;, to take out of them a people for his name.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="1 Corinthians 15:6; Acts 21:20; Revelation 3:4"> And to this agree the words of the prophets; as it is written,</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Acts 1:25; Mark 3:19; John 17:12"> That the residue (part that remains, the rest) of men might seek after the Lord - Kurios, and all the Goyim &#91;Gentiles&#93;, upon whom my name is called, says the Lord - Kurios, who does all these things.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Gosel 27:3–10; Gosel 26:14–15; Tehillim - Psalms 55:23"> Known unto God-The Father are all his works from the beginning of the world.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Acts 2:22; Gosel 28:15; Acts 21:40"> For what reason my sentence is, that we trouble not them, which from among the Goyim &#91;Gentiles&#93; are turned to God-The Father:</span>

<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 69:25; Tehillim - Psalms 109:8–15; Acts 1:25"> But that we write unto them, that they abstain from pollutions of idols, and &#91;from&#93; fornication, and &#91;from&#93; things strangled, and &#91;from&#93; blood.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="John 15:27; Luke 10:1–2; Numbers 27:17"> For Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; of old time has in every city them that preach him, being read in the synagogues every Shabbat &#91;rest&#93; day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Acts 1:2; Acts 4:33; Acts 1:8–9"> Then pleased it the apostles and elders, with the whole church, to send chosen men of their own company to Antioch &#91;speedy as a chariot&#93; with Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; and Bar-Nabba &#91;son of rest&#93;; &#91;namely&#93;, Y’hudah &#91;praised&#93; surnamed Bar-Sabba &#91;Son of consolation or son of Exhortation (encouragement; counsel)&#93;, and Silas &#91;woody&#93;, chief men among the brethren:</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Acts 15:22; Acts 1:26"> And they wrote &#91;letters&#93; by them after this manner; The apostles and elders and brethren &#91;send&#93; greeting unto the brethren which are of the Goyim &#91;Gentiles&#93; in Antioch &#91;speedy as a chariot&#93; and Aram &#91;exalted&#93; (Syria &#91;etymology is uncertain&#93;) and Cilicia &#91;the land which rolls&#93;:</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="1 Samuel 16:7; Acts 15:8; Hebrews 4:13"> Forasmuch as we have heard, that certain which went out from us have troubled you with words, subverting your souls, saying, &#91;You 2f must&#93; be circumcised, and keep the law: to whom we gave no &#91;such&#93; commandment:</span>

<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="1 Corinthians 9:2; John 6:70–71; John 17:12"> It seemed good unto us, being assembled with one accord, to send chosen men unto you with our beloved Bar-Nabba &#91;son of rest&#93; and Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93;,</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> We have sent therefore Y’hudah &#91;praised&#93; and Silas &#91;woody&#93;, who shall also tell &#91;you&#93; the same things by mouth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> For it seemed good to the Holy Spirit &#91;Ruach HaKodesh&#93;, and to us, to lay upon you no greater burden than these necessary things;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> That you2f abstain from meats offered to idols, and from blood, and from things strangled, and from fornication: from which if you2f keep yourselves, you2f shall do well. Fare you2f well.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> So when they were dismissed, they came to Antioch &#91;speedy as a chariot&#93;: and when they had gathered the multitude together, they delivered the Epistle (important or formal letter):</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> &#91;Which&#93; when they had read, they rejoiced for the consolation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> And Y’hudah &#91;praised&#93; and Silas &#91;woody&#93;, being prophets also themselves, exhorted the brethren with many words, and confirmed &#91;them&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And after they had tarried &#91;there&#93; a space, they were let go in peace from the brethren unto the apostles.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> Yet it pleased Silas &#91;woody&#93; to abide there still.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; also and Bar-Nabba &#91;son of rest&#93; continued in Antioch &#91;speedy as a chariot&#93;, teaching and preaching the word of the Lord - Kurios, with many others also.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> And some days after Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; said unto Bar-Nabba &#91;son of rest&#93;, Let us go again and visit our brethren in every city where we have preached the word of the Lord - Kurios, &#91;and see&#93; how they do.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> And Bar-Nabba &#91;son of rest&#93; determined to take with them Yochanan &#91;Yehovah has graced&#93;, whose surname was Mark &#91;a defense&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> But Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; thought not good to take him with them, who departed from them from Pamphylia &#91;of every tribe&#93;, and went not with them to the work.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39"> And the contention was so sharp between them, that they departed asunder one from the other: and so Bar-Nabba &#91;son of rest&#93; took Mark &#91;a defense&#93;, and sailed unto Cyprus &#91;love: a blossom&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40"> And Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; chose Silas &#91;woody&#93;, and departed, being recommended by the brethren unto the grace of God-The Father.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41"> And he went through Aram &#91;exalted&#93; (Syria &#91;etymology is uncertain&#93;) and Cilicia &#91;the land which rolls&#93;, confirming the churches.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Luke 1:3; Luke 24:19; Gosel 4:23–24"> Then came he to Derbe &#91;tanner of skin&#93; and Lystra &#91;ransoming&#93;: and, behold, a certain disciple was there, named Timothy &#91;honoring God&#93;, the son of a certain woman, which was a Jewish person, and believed; but his father &#91;was&#93; a Greek &#91;unstable: the miry one&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Acts 1:9; John 20:21; Isaiah 59:20–21"> Which was well reported of by the brethren that were at Lystra &#91;ransoming&#93; and Iconium &#91;little image&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="1 Corinthians 15:5–7; Acts 13:31; John 20:26"> Him would Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; have to go forth with him; and took and circumcised him because of the Y’hudim (Judeans) &#91;praised of Yah&#93; which were in those quarters: for they knew all that his father was a Greek &#91;unstable: the miry one&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Luke 24:49; John 14:16; John 15:26"> And as they went through the cities, they delivered them the decrees for to keep, that were ordained of the apostles and elders which were at Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Gosel 3:11; Acts 11:15–16; Luke 3:16"> And so were the churches established in the faith, and increased in number daily.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Luke 19:11; Luke 17:20; Daniel 7:27"> Now when they had gone throughout Phrygia &#91;dry, barren&#93; and the region of Galatia &#91;land of the Galli, Gauls, A Celt&#93;, and were forbidden of the Holy Spirit &#91;Ruach HaKodesh&#93; to preach the word in Asia &#91;orient&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Gosel 24:36; 1 Thessalonians 5:1–2; Mark 13:32"> After they were come to Mysia &#91;land of beach trees&#93;, they assayed (attempted; tested; analyzed) to go into Bithynia: but the Spirit &#91;Ruach&#93; suffered them not.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Gosel 28:19; Mark 16:15; Romans 15:19"> And they passing by Mysia &#91;land of beach trees&#93; came down to Troas (Trojan) &#91;penetrated&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Daniel 7:13; Luke 24:50–51; Mark 16:19"> And a vision appeared to Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; in the night; There stood a man of Macedonia &#91;extended land&#93;, and prayed him, saying, Come over into Macedonia &#91;extended land&#93;, and help us.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Luke 24:4; John 20:12; Revelation 7:14"> And after he had seen the vision, immediately we endeavoured to go into Macedonia &#91;extended land&#93;, assuredly gathering that the Lord - Kurios had called us for to preach the Gospel (joyful message) unto them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Revelation 1:7; 1 Thessalonians 4:16; Mark 13:26"> Therefore loosing from Troas (Trojan) &#91;penetrated&#93;, we came with a straight course to Samothracia &#91;a sign of rags&#93;, and the next &#91;day&#93; to Neapolis &#91;new city&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Luke 24:52; Gosel 21:1; Luke 24:50"> And from there to Philippi &#91;lover of horses&#93;, which is the chief city of that part of Macedonia &#91;extended land&#93;, &#91;and&#93; a colony: and we were in that city abiding certain days.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Acts 20:8; Luke 22:12; Mark 14:15"> And on the Shabbat &#91;rest&#93; we went out of the city by a river side, where prayer was wont (used to; in the habit of) to be made; and we sat down, and spoke unto the women which resorted &#91;there&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Acts 6:4; Ephesians 6:18; Colossians 4:2"> And a certain woman named Lydia &#91;travail&#93;, a seller of purple, of the city of Thyatira &#91;odour of affliction&#93;, which worshipped God-The Father, heard &#91;us&#93;: whose heart the Lord - Kurios opened, that she attended unto the things which were spoken of Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93;.</span>

<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Gosel 3:11; Acts 11:15–16; Luke 3:16"> Then said Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93;, I did not know not, brethren, that he was the High Priest: for it is written, youi shall not speak evil of the ruler of youri people.</span>

<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Luke 19:11; Luke 17:20; Daniel 7:27"> But when Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; perceived that the one part were Tz’dukim (Sadducees) &#91;followers of the right&#93;, and the other P’rushim (Pharisee &#91;separatist&#93;s) &#91;separatists&#93;, he cried out in the council, Men &#91;and&#93; brethren, I am a Pharisee &#91;separatist&#93;, the son of a Pharisee &#91;separatist&#93;: of the hope and resurrection of the dead I am called in question.</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Daniel 7:13; Luke 24:50–51; Mark 16:19"> And there arose a great cry: and the Torah-teachers (Scribes) &#91;that were&#93; of the P’rushim (Pharisee &#91;separatist&#93;s) &#91;separatists&#93;’ part arose, and strove, saying, We find no evil in this man: but if a Spirit &#91;Ruach&#93; or an angel has spoken to him, let us not fight against God-The Father.</span>

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Luke 1:3; Luke 24:19; Gosel 4:23–24"> And when they were escaped, then they knew that the island was called Melita (Malta) &#91;honey&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Acts 1:9; John 20:21; Isaiah 59:20–21"> And the barbarous (foreign) people showed us exceptional kindness: for they kindled a fire, and received us every one, because of the present rain, and because of the cold.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="1 Corinthians 15:5–7; Acts 13:31; John 20:26"> And when Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; had gathered a bundle of sticks, and laid &#91;them&#93; on the fire, there came a viper out of the heat, and fastened on his hand.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Luke 24:49; John 14:16; John 15:26"> And when the barbarians saw the &#91;venomous&#93; beast hang on his hand, they said among themselves, No doubt this man is a murderer, whom, though he has escaped the sea, yet vengeance suffers not to live.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Gosel 3:11; Acts 11:15–16; Luke 3:16"> And he shook off the beast into the fire, and felt no harm.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Luke 19:11; Luke 17:20; Daniel 7:27"> However they looked when he should have swollen, or fallen down dead suddenly: but after they had looked a great while, and saw no harm come to him, they changed their minds, and said that he was a god.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Gosel 24:36; 1 Thessalonians 5:1–2; Mark 13:32"> In the same quarters were possessions of the chief man of the island, whose name was Publius &#91;popular&#93;; who received us, and lodged us three days courteously.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Gosel 28:19; Mark 16:15; Romans 15:19"> And it came to pass, that the father of Publius &#91;popular&#93; lay sick of a fever and of a bloody flux (bloody flow from the body; dysentery): to whom Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; entered in, and prayed, and laid his hands on him, and healed him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Daniel 7:13; Luke 24:50–51; Mark 16:19"> So when this was done, others also, which had diseases in the island, came, and were healed:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Luke 24:4; John 20:12; Revelation 7:14"> Who also honoured us with many honours; and when we departed, they laded &#91;us&#93; with such things as were necessary.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Revelation 1:7; 1 Thessalonians 4:16; Mark 13:26"> And after three months we departed in a ship of Alexandria &#91;one who assists men&#93;, which had wintered in the isle, whose sign was Castor &#91;beaver&#93; and Pollux &#91;crown&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Luke 24:52; Gosel 21:1; Luke 24:50"> And landing at Syracuse &#91;a Syrian hearing&#93;, we tarried &#91;there&#93; three days.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Acts 20:8; Luke 22:12; Mark 14:15"> And from there we went on a circular course (go around, encircle; encompass), and came to Rhegium &#91;breach (breaking forth; a break; opening, inlet)&#93;: and after one day the south wind blew, and we came the next day to Puteoli &#91;sulphurous springs&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Acts 6:4; Ephesians 6:18; Colossians 4:2"> Where we found brethren, and were desired to wait with them seven days: and so we went toward Rome &#91;strength&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="1 Corinthians 15:6; Acts 21:20; Revelation 3:4"> And from there, when the brethren heard of us, they came to meet us as far as Appii &#91;The Market Place Appius&#93; Forum, and The Three Taverns: whom when Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; saw, he thanked God-The Father, and took courage.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 41:9; John 13:18; Tehillim - Psalms 55:12–15"> And when we came to Rome &#91;strength&#93;, the Centurion (Roman commander of an hundred men) delivered the prisoners to the captain of the guard: but Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; was suffered to dwell by himself with a soldier that kept him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Acts 1:25; Mark 3:19; John 17:12"> And it came to pass, that after three days Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; called the chief of the Y’hudim (Judeans) &#91;praised of Yah&#93; together: and when they were come together, he said unto them, Men &#91;and&#93; brethren, though I have committed nothing against the people, or customs of our fathers, yet was I delivered prisoner from Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; into the hands of the Romans &#91;strength&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Gosel 27:3–10; Gosel 26:14–15; Tehillim - Psalms 55:23"> Who, when they had examined me, would have let &#91;me&#93; go, because there was no cause of death in me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Acts 2:22; Gosel 28:15; Acts 21:40"> But when the Y’hudim (Judeans) &#91;praised of Yah&#93; spoke against &#91;it&#93;, I was constrained to appeal unto Caesar &#91;venerable&#93;; not that I had ought to accuse my nation of.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 69:25; Tehillim - Psalms 109:8–15; Acts 1:25"> For this cause therefore have I called for you, to see &#91;you&#93;, and to speak with &#91;you&#93;: because that for the hope of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heal Of God&#93; I am bound with this chain.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="John 15:27; Luke 10:1–2; Numbers 27:17"> And they said unto him, We neither received letters out of Y’hudah &#91;praised&#93; concerning youi, neither any of the brethren that came showed or spoke any harm of youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Acts 1:2; Acts 4:33; Acts 1:8–9"> But we desire to hear of youi what youi think: for as concerning this sect, we know that every where it is spoken against.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Acts 15:22; Acts 1:26"> And when they had appointed him a day, there came many to him into &#91;his&#93; lodging; to whom he expounded and testified the kingdom of God-The Father, persuading them concerning Yeshua &#91;God is Salvation&#93;, both out of the law of Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, and &#91;out of&#93; the prophets, from morning till evening.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="1 Samuel 16:7; Acts 15:8; Hebrews 4:13"> And some believed the things which were spoken, and some believed not.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="1 Corinthians 9:2; John 6:70–71; John 17:12"> And when they agreed not among themselves, they departed, after that Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; had spoken one word, Well spoke the Holy Spirit &#91;Ruach HaKodesh&#93; by Yesha’yahu &#91;Yah is salvation&#93; the prophet unto our fathers,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Leviticus 16:8; Joshua 18:10; 1 Samuel 14:41–42"> Saying, Go unto this people, and say, Hearing you2f shall hear, and shall not understand; and seeing you2f shall see, and not perceive:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> For the heart of this people is waxed gross, and their ears are dull of hearing, and their eyes have they closed; lest they should see with &#91;their&#93; eyes, and hear with &#91;their&#93; ears, and understand with &#91;their&#93; heart, and should be converted, and I should heal them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> Be it known therefore unto you, that the salvation of God-The Father is sent unto the Goyim &#91;Gentiles&#93;, and &#91;that&#93; they will hear it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> And when he had said these words, the Y’hudim (Judeans) &#91;praised of Yah&#93; departed, and had great reasoning among themselves.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> And Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; dwelt two whole years in his own hired house, and received all that came in unto him,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> Preaching the kingdom of God-The Father, and teaching those things which concern the Lord - Kurios &#91;he to whom a person belongs&#93; Yeshua &#91;God is Salvation&#93; Moshiach &#91;Messiah&#93;, with all confidence, no man forbidding him.</span>
</div>
</div>

</div>
